Report: critics/reports/c566.md

Accepted: timpani "damp" on the empty bar 4, before the muta at 5. The A♭2 drum would otherwise ring in sympathy with the cellos' and bass clarinet's A♭2 and slide audibly when pedalled to C3 in the quiet string-free bars (Adler part 2 PDF 80-81, Ex. XII-51). Text only; MIDI byte-identical; matches the damps at 53, 62 and 86.

Closed (no change): c436's retune of the C and D♭ drums during 87-91. The pedals would pass through sounding pitches in the quietest bars, and the scherzo needs only E2 by its bar 3, so the muta stays in the pause at 92.

Recorded (no change): drum layout, stick changes, roll endings and ringing strokes.

## v399 (cycle 398, expression; critic c398, Adler ch. 2 bowing, PDF 36-55)
- Applied (bowing marks): Vln II and violas take the 64 b3 pickup up-bow and change to a down-bow on the tied note at 66, so the fade to niente is no longer about 9.8 s on one bow (PDF 38-40, Ex. II-20; Dvorak 9/ii does the same). This supersedes c278/c376's "one bow" record.
- Applied (bowing marks): cellos change bow on the tied E flat at 58 b4 and take 59 in one bow, so the f arrival at 59 is a down-bow like the other strings, 60 an up-bow crescendo and 61 ff a down-bow (answers c096: no two long up-bows in a row).
- Recorded, no change: Vln II crescendo on a down-bow at 52; six-beat holds at 2-3; violin diminuendos on up-bows at 58 and 82; viola arpeggiando and notes per bow. score.midi byte-identical.

# v184 — cycle 183 (lens 3 form, Aldwell & Schachter Unit 14 V as a Key Area, PDF 211–229, second pass)

No change (v184 = v183 relabelled). Critic report: critics/reports/c183.md. B♭ minor (45–46) and A minor (63–64) are tonicizations; F♯ minor (47–51) has preparation (C♯7), confirmation (iv–N6–V–i at 49–50) and return (G♯7 = A♭7 of D♭ at 51). The retransition 65–70 follows PDF 220. V is never a key area, as in Dvořák 9/ii; the half cadences are graded (10, 26 plain; 22 chromatic). Bar 67's missing seventh was considered and kept (c174 cleared that bar for the horn entry).

---

# v183 — cycle 182 (lens 2 harmony, Aldwell & Schachter Unit 5 Procedures of Four-Part Writing, PDF 74–91, second pass)

No change (v183 = v182 relabelled). Critic report: critics/reports/c182.md. The critic built a per-player MIDI and checked parallels, hidden outer-voice fifths and octaves, crossing, spacing and doubled leading tones and sevenths in 1–6, 15–22, 31–36, 61–70 and 79–92. Every parallel found is one line doubled in two instruments. Recorded exceptions: the four-octave G♯ at 62–63 (tutti emphasis, as Dvořák 9/ii; the only fix would sit in Tbn 1's quotation); the wide gaps at 21–22, 27–28 and 75–76 (two chords each); IV6 at 87 with four Fs (the protected F–F♭–E♭ line); cello E2→B♭2 at 64–65 into the dominant seventh.

# v182 — cycle 181 (lens 1 melody, Aldwell & Schachter Unit 20 Melodic Figuration, PDF 327–345, second pass)

No change (v182 = v181 relabelled). Critic report: critics/reports/c181.md. The critic checked the figuration of themes A and B and both climaxes against Unit 20: the 79–81 B♭6 is an accented upper neighbour over IV that moves 6–5 to the structural A♭6; the theme B consequent at 57–61 is a complete 5̂–1̂ descent; A♭6 (58, 61–62) – A♮6 (63) – B♭6 (79) is a long-range chromatic line linking the two climaxes. Bar 58's missing neighbour figure was considered and rejected (it would need the B♭6 kept for 79). Nothing affects the ending or the exported themes.

# v171 — cycle 170 (lens 10 cyclic, Aldwell & Schachter Unit 23 mixture as a long-range device, PDF 386–397, second pass; compared with movements I and III)
Critic report: critics/reports/c170.md. Finding 7 accepted (coda.ily header comments only; MIDI byte-identical); 1–2, 5–6, 8 no change; 3–4 relayed.

1. Long-range mixture: every F♭/E in the A♭ sections goes on to E♭; 3–6 and 87–89 frame the movement with the same plagal ♭6̂ neighbour.
2. Handover to III: II's last chord + F♯ = III's German sixth (A&S Ex. 23-8); III's augmented theme B recall now ≈13 s.
3. Relay: III could close C–A♭–E–C with one I–III♯–V in its C-major pages.
4. Correction for cyclic-notes: at 78 the viola's F♭ resolves through the bass and harp, not in its own instrument (correct as written).
5. Cross relation Vln II F4 against bass F♭ at 21: accepted exception.

# v163 — cycle 162 (lens 2 harmony, Aldwell & Schachter Units 30–31, PDF 530–583, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c162.md. No change (v163 = v162 relabelled).
- Bar 33's chord is a passing diminished seventh (parallel sixths under the motto, PDF 552), not a common-tone diminished seventh. Future critics should not ask it to resolve upward.
- The climax 62–65 follows A&S's Schubert Quintet model (Ex. 30-9, PDF 535–537), which is an added reason for its protection.
- Bass G♭–G–A♭ at 59, coda 85–88, spellings: follow the reading.
- Relay at cycle 170: movements I and II outline C–A♭–F♭/E (major-third division of the octave); movement III could close it A♭→E→C.

1. Bar 21 b3: French sixth F♭–A♭–B♭–D (Vc/Cb to F♭, Vln II B♭3 tied to 22) after the deceptive cadence, opening to the E♭ octave at 22 (A&S PDF 506–513). Removes c132's cross relation at 21 and brings F♭→E♭ into the first A section.

Recorded, no change: enharmonic pivot at 50–52; ♭VI→IV at 78–79; Tbn F over E at 64; French sixth at 68 (rejected at c089).

# v152 — cycle 151 (lens 1 melody, Aldwell & Schachter Unit 21 rhythmic figuration, PDF 346–371, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c151.md. Findings 1–2 accepted; 3 rejected.

1. Bar 58 b4: Hn 1 and cellos hold E♭ and move to F on beat 4½, so the resolution note does not sound under the G♭–F suspension early (A&S PDF 352, 365–366). Settles the c082/c089 back-and-forth.
2. Bar 58 b2: melody aes4. ges8~ ges4~ ges8 f8 in Fl 1, Ob 1, Vln I, Vln II, so the suspension falls on the stronger beat (PDF 347, 369). A♭6 ceiling kept; both octaves match.
3. Rejected: Cl 2 bes'4. ges'8 (one eighth of contrast-register clash is acceptable; PDF 352).

# v151 — cycle 150 (lens 10 cyclic, Rimsky-Korsakov prefaces and transfer of phrases, PDF 13–23, 125–128, second pass; compared with movement I v220 and movement III)
Critic report: critics/reports/c150.md. Findings 1–2 accepted; 3 no change; 4 relayed; 5 kept.

1. Bars 87–90: cor anglais resolves F♭4 to E♭4 under its slur, holds it as Cl 2 enters at 89 and fades at 90 with the muted horns, so every F♭ resolves in its own instrument (cyclic-notes) and the handover shares a note (Rimsky PDF 125–128).
2. Timpani part, last bar: part-only "muta D♭ in G (for III, during the pause)".
4. Handover to movement III relayed (bar 1 German sixth layout, violin peaks, horn mutes; mvt III v04 lacks a starting harp pedal diagram).

# v150 — cycle 149 (lens 9 holistic vs Schumann 2/iii; Rimsky-Korsakov examples 275–312, PDF 441–504, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c149.md. Findings 1–2 accepted; 3–4 no change.

1. Bar 78: the grace run into B♭6 written out (c'''8( \tuplet 3/2 { f'''16 g''' aes''' }) in Fl 1, Vln I and Vln II, since doubled runs must be measured (Rimsky PDF 141; Ex. 283, 304). Pitches and slurs unchanged.
2. Bars 33–36: Bsn 2 leaves after 33 b2 so it no longer sits in unison with the bass clarinet's A♭2; the contrabassoon holds the A♭1 pedal through 36 (PDF 139–141). Solo and the chord at 33 unchanged.
3–4. Hn 4 doubling the violas at 87–88; accompaniment at 7–14: kept (protected).

# v149 — cycle 148 (lens 8 expression, Aldwell & Schachter Unit 3 rhythm and metre, PDF 47–57, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c148.md. Findings 1–3 accepted (articulation only).

2. Bars 80–81: tenutos on the hemiola attacks (B♭ at 80 b3, D♭ at 81 b2) in Vc, Hn 1·2, Bsn 1 (PDF 50–52).
3. Bar 88: Cl 1 des''2.-- (soft re-tongue under the slur) (PDF 55; c126).

# v148 — cycle 147 (lens 7 texture, Rimsky-Korsakov ch. III overlaying, crossing, enclosure, PDF 106–114, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c147.md. Finding 1 accepted; 2–3 no change (3 closed).

2. Cl 2 F4 with Hn 3 at 79 b1: kept (one beat; c123's choice).
3. Hn 4 E♭3 enclosed by strings at 29–30: kept (pp, two bars; as c047). Closed.

# v147 — cycle 146 (lens 6 playability, Adler ch. 12 timpani, PDF 55–91, second pass)

3. "near the rim" at 67, "ord." at 79 (PDF 80). Closes the c056 item.
4. Rejected: moving the retuning from 5 to 37 (playable; settled at c056).
5. Roll notation: three-stroke slash kept; item closed.

# v146 — cycle 145 (lens 5 engraving, Adler ch. 18 and App. A, PDF 291–312, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c145.md. Findings 1–5 accepted (MIDI identical).

# v143 — cycle 142 (lens 2 harmony, Aldwell & Schachter Units 24–25, PDF 398–441, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c142.md. Findings 1–2 accepted (fixing side effects of c137 and c141); 3–4 recorded.

# v142 — cycle 141 (lens 1 melody, Sebesky ch. 6–7, PDF 204–230, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c141.md. Finding 1 accepted; 2 closes c131's note; 3–5 no change.

1. Bars 37–40: Vln II held chord re-voiced (<des' aes'>1 | <des' bes'~>2 <ges' bes'~>2 | <des' bes'~>2 <ges' bes'>2 | <es' ges'>2 <des' f'>2), so it no longer doubles the flute's tune an octave below and its top line moves (Sebesky PDF 210–212, 219). Pitches only, so the c124 rule holds. It still sustains chord tones of the viola arpeggio (c137).
2. Theme B's narrow first phrase matches Dvořák 9/ii's middle tune plan; c131's note closed.
3. Hn/Cl counter-line touching theme notes at 54–56: brief, kept.
4. Bsn 1 re-striking the horn's A♭3 at 11: no clean fix; recorded.
5. Theme A and climax plan: no change.

# v141 — cycle 140 (lens 10 cyclic, Adler ch. 1 and ch. 15 close, adler1 PDF 19–23, adler2 PDF 187–196, second pass; compared with movement I v220)
Critic report: critics/reports/c140.md. Finding 1 accepted; 3 comment fix accepted; 2, 3 and 5 relayed; 4 and 6 no change.

1. Bars 31–37: bass clarinet solo an octave lower (chalumeau), so the motto tail sounds E♭3–D3–D♭3–C3, the pitch and instrument of movement I's opening motto and coda bass (Adler part 1 PDF 217; part 2 PDF 188). Cor anglais moves down with it (C4–A♭3), still a sixth above; Bsn 1 enters at 33 b2 on F. Ends on D♭3 at 37. Winds alone, the A♭ pedal and the diminished seventh at 33 are kept, so this is a deliberate exception to the choir's protection.
3. intro.ily comment: Horn 3 holds about 13 s before bar 1 (movement I cycle 140).
4. CA/Fl shared F5 at 37 kept (the alternative makes 9 pages).
2, 3, 5: relayed to the coordinator (movement III points, Horn 3 and string page turns at the attacca, record corrections).

# v140 — cycle 139 (lens 9 holistic vs Brahms 4/ii; Rimsky-Korsakov examples 206–274, PDF 376–440, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c139.md. Findings 1–2 accepted; 3 rejected (9 pages).

2. Bars 17–18, 21: Vln II hairpins under the solo violin removed (c138 partly undone); swells under the oboe at 15–16, 19–20 kept (PDF 123, 133).

2. Viola triplets on counter-line notes at 57–60: kept (inside the crescendo).

# v121 — cycle 120 (lens 10 cyclic, Aldwell & Schachter Unit 26, PDF 442–461, second pass; compared with movement I v220)

# v116 — cycle 115 (lens 5 engraving, Gould ch. 1, PDF 41–52, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c115.md. Findings 1–4 accepted (engraving only, MIDI unchanged); 5–6 no change.

2. Harp LH 14: octave line ends just after the chord (PDF 44).
3. Flutes 1·2 at 63: one shared rest set in the score (flute 2 spacer, tagged; parts keep R1) (PDF 51).
4. Horns 1·2 at 70: horn 2 spacer in the score, horn 1 rest centred (PDF 49, 51). score.ly: \fluteTwo and \hnTwo use \removeWithTag #'part.
5. Bassoon 1 rests 57–58 kept; 6. ledger lines within limits, no octave signs needed (PDF 47).

# v115 — cycle 114 (lens 4 orchestration, Rimsky-Korsakov tutti-chord appendix PDF 505–510 and ch. IV examples, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c114.md. Finding 1 accepted; 2–4 no change.

1. Harp LH at 74, 77, 78: octaves in the lowest register (B♭1–B♭2, E♭1–E♭2) instead of a fourth, a fifth and a sixth (Rimsky-Korsakov PDF 124–125), matching 9, 10, 13 and c089's fixes at 8, 13, 72, 73.
2. Tutti chord 61–62 kept (appendix chords 1–4, Ex. 209).
3. Release 63–65 kept (PDF 115–116).
4. No string chord on the 79 downbeat (grace run arrives there; PDF 130).

# v114 — cycle 113 (lens 3 form, Aldwell & Schachter Unit 32, PDF 584–606, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c113.md. No change: all six findings confirm the current score (v114 is v113 relabelled).

1. 61–65 follows the ♭VI → minor ♭II → V7 route of the Widmung example (PDF 593).
2. E7→E♭7 at 64–65 is a chromatic slide, not a German sixth; kept (D♭4 is the protected dominant seventh).
3. Remote pitches planted early and resolved in the top voice (PDF 597–598).
4. 77–82 follows Mozart K. 542's deceptive-cadence pattern (PDF 601–602).
5. Proportions match Dvořák 9/ii; peak at 84% of the duration.
6. Enharmonic spellings at 47–50 and 62–64 kept (PDF 588).

# v113 — cycle 112 (lens 2 harmony, Aldwell & Schachter Units 11–13, PDF 163–210)
Critic report: critics/reports/c112.md. Findings 1–2 accepted; 3–5 no change.

1. Bar 55: ii7 now moves to a proper dominant approach instead of vi6. Vc/Cb bass E♭–C at beat 3, then A, B♭; harp RH B♭–E♭–G♭; Bsn 1 resolves its 7th D♭3 to C3 (stem down to keep 8 pages). Cl 1/Hn 1 counter-line holds G♭4 through 55 (one change to the protected line; F4 would clash). Closes the long-open "bar 55 II harmony" item.
2. Bar 43 beats 1–2: violas D♭4 instead of E♭4, making II6/5 before the cadential 6/4; D♭ prepared at 42 and resolved to C4. Removes the viola/cor anglais unison on E♭4.
3. IV to vi7 at 41 and 57: kept (protected lines; a fix at 41 makes outer-voice fifths).
4. IV7 seventh entering by leap at 25: kept (free texture, protected cello counter-melody).
5. Hn 4 octave doubling of the resolving 7th at 80: kept (c022).
Score 8 pages, 0 warnings; parts book 0 warnings.

# v112 — cycle 111 (lens 1 melody, Rimsky-Korsakov ch. II Melody, PDF 64–80, second pass)
Critic report: critics/reports/c111.md. Findings 1–2 accepted (sections/trans.ily, cor anglais line only).

1. Bars 32–34: cor anglais up an octave, pp, now a sixth above the bass clarinet on the motto tail (oboe family above clarinet family; Rimsky-Korsakov PDF 65, 67, 70). Harmony of the 31–36 choir unchanged; this is a deliberate exception to its do-not-touch entry.
2. Bar 36: cor anglais pickup C5 on beat 3 into theme B at 37, matching the rising fourth at 44 and 52. Score stays 8 pages (the flute pickup still made 9).
Score 8 pages, 0 warnings; parts book rebuilt, 0 warnings.

Score 8 pages, 0 warnings; parts book 0 warnings.
